h1. SURPRISE Samples

This page lists all MCC9.10 samples produced by the DM team. If you have questions on any of the samples, or believe any of the information here is incorrect, please contact the DM conveners (Ben Bogart bbogart@fnal.gov). This page is up to date through May 11th, 2026. All "wc processed" files were up updated June 21st, 2026 to enure consistent treatment of subruns where the Lantern container failed, incorporate spline weights into samples, and ensure consistent treatment of simulated zero-lifetime period in several of the overlay samples. 

The most up to date version of the workflows can be found on the "super unified workflow wiki page":https://cdcvs.fnal.gov/redmine/projects/uboone-physics-analysis/wiki/Super_Unified_Workflow_. These may be newer than the workflows used to produce these samples, see the "production spreadsheet":https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/12Kd2GsfRDqKliq6KT2xAyzqiYpNyYWqpV3gih4Ee01s/edit?gid=367830408#gid=367830408 to verify the workflow used during production. Note that the "unprocessed" files have not had the good runs list applied. 

Additional details on the Wire-Cell processing as well as resources to help deal with the large size of these files can be found in the "Wire-Cell processing wiki page":https://cdcvs.fnal.gov/redmine/projects/uboone-physics-analysis/wiki/Wcpuboonebdt.
